This Directory is published and distributed as a service to the 

Section of Administrative Law of the American Bar Association 

by The Bureau of National Affairs, Inc. 

promptly and

A private organization of information specialists in the Nation's Capital, BNA provides a wide range of notification and reference services and related materials for professional and business use. One of BNA's many specialized information services is 

The United States Law Week, which keeps lawyers posted accurately on current court decisions and federal agency rulings that make new law. In addition to a Summary and Analysis of leading cases of the week and other current developments of legal significance, this service provides fast, complete coverage of the Supreme Court - including full text of all opinions, printed and mailed the same day they are handed down. Law Week is carefully organized and fully indexed, and thus serves as a valuable research tool for the bench and the bar. 

BNA publishes the following daily, weekly, biweekly, and monthly reports and services:
